- **context**: Load a context primer for the current repo before starting work. Use when the work touches a run, when the user says "keep going", "a task", or "the loop", or when they ask "context-budget", "how do I keep the working set small enough to stay coherent", or "context budget keeps failing". Also use when a run must be produced, reviewed, or repaired, or when a previous attempt left it unproven.
